Updated 11 October 2026. Word editing/rendering is deferred by user until implementation completes; original report unchanged. Do not resume report QA now.

Approved: all nine final modules; Expo patient Android UI, Next.js dashboard, ONE shared Python backend; Supabase auth/database/private storage planned but no hosted environment selected or accessed. Preview requires skin assessment/history, booking, queue, prescriptions and billing with shared records; AI/payments simulated. Patients see own records; doctors explicit/booking-authorised clinical access; staff minimum operational details; admins no automatic clinical access. Common conditions; no dataset/model; meaningful fine-tuning/evaluation required. Prefer concise updates and one selection card at a time; keep turn active while awaiting answers.

First increment starts from main 87ad49d on feat/booking-queue. apps/mobile, apps/dashboard, apps/api and packages/shared now exist. FastAPI owns transactions; ignored apps/api/data/demo.sqlite3 saves synthetic records across restarts. Demo headers are selectable identities, NOT secure authentication.

Implemented: availability, booking confirmation/retry keys, duplicate/capacity protection, own history, cancellation, queue filters/call-next/completion and shared periodic refresh. Order: appointment time then token; one called patient per doctor/date. Cancellation frees capacity, keeps history/token. Staff can rehearse future clinic dates.

Six API tests and both TypeScript checks passed. Further results in VERIFY.md. Physical Android pending. Camera/AI, prescriptions/billing and real auth are not implemented. Report helper scripts are historical unfinished work, not app features.

Next: finish phone booking/queue checks, then prescription/dispensing/billing tied to saved visits, followed by photo/history with simulated inference. All nine final modules remain required. SETUP.md has launchers. No remote push, deployment, paid service, hosted mutation or private photo upload authorised. Keep synthetic data only. Never claim phone checks, full report audit or model accuracy without evidence. Save local checkpoints after verified increments.

## Skin assessment increment — 12 October 2026

Started from 13dee42 on feat/skin-assessment. Added Expo image-picker ~57.0.20/file-system ~57.0.7, Python multipart0.0.32/Pillow12.3.0. Skin check supports camera permission/recovery, gallery, preview/discard/confirmation, saved labelled simulation and latest100 own-history entries. Unsaved native draft lives in cache and is removed on discard/replacement/save/unmount. Confirmed photos live as normalized JPEG BLOBs in the shared ignored SQLite database, so history is independent of temporary picker paths. No external image transfers, trained model or doctor assessment access implemented.

Backend validates actual readable JPEG/PNG/WebP bytes, max5MB, dimensions32px minimum/25MP maximum, resizes longest side1600 and strips source metadata/EXIF/GPS. Photo and metadata commit atomically; per-patient request keys/hash prevent duplicate retry or mismatched reuse. Patients retrieve only own assessment metadata/photo; staff/doctor roles denied. Every response says simulation and no image analysis; fixed acne example is not prediction or diagnosis, no confidence score.

## Small increment: appointment rescheduling — 12 October 2026

From8207f2c on feat/appointment-rescheduling. Patient My visits -> Reschedule [token] -> choose future time -> confirm; staff Patient queue -> Reschedule [token] -> time -> Review -> Confirm. Only own patient or staff may move a future waiting visit. The transaction creates a new waiting booking with a fresh doctor/day queue number and marks the old booking cancelled with a rescheduled_to_id history indicator. Capacity, duplicate prevention, old/new status and access updates commit together. Failure preserves the original visit. Actor/request keys make exact retries return the saved replacement; changed targets conflict. Concurrent moves cannot move the old visit twice.

Active appointment-linked assessment grants move to the replacement only for the same doctor. A doctor change revokes these grants and requires a fresh patient link; direct shares stay active. Revoked grant history/published reviews remain retained. No automatic sharing with a new doctor. Consultation/dispensing/billing rules unchanged; called/completed visits cannot be moved.

From99f686f on feat/slot-duration. Staff Queue -> Appointment slots adds slot duration15–120min in15min steps, default30, reviews start/end + duration/capacity before confirm. Duration represents the clinic slot/session window, capacity remains patients within that window; not a separate per-patient duration. API checks same-doctor open windows under BEGIN IMMEDIATE with half-open interval overlap rules: touching endpoints allowed, partial/contained/exact overlaps rejected, different doctors independent, crosses midnight handled. Closed empty slots release their windows for different starts; same doctor's identical start remains blocked even for closed slots (timezone-normalized). Existing booked slots cannot close as before. No duration editing/retiming existing slots.
Additive slots.duration_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT30 with15..120/multiple15 CHECK. Existing starts/capacity/bookings preserved, prior durations inferred30; migration does not reconstruct actual historical consultation lengths. Initial live audit35 open slots had0overlaps at30min. Ends are derived from start+duration, not separately stored. API public/managed slots and Booking include duration/end; idempotent creation request compares duration too. Seed now serialized and skips existing IDs/exactstarts and overlapping staff windows, preserving closed rows/durations on restart. No new overlapping defaults inserted on restart; no automatic legacy conflict repair.
Shared visitWindow formats Malaysia start/end, shows date on cross-midnight end. Staff creation/list/confirmation, counterbooking, patient matching/booking confirmation, follow-up time selection, both reschedule UIs and patient visit-details header show time windows. Existing queue token/access/capacity rules unchanged; legacy cached Booking can fall back to start display. No clinical-record editing or new sharing.

## Correct a missed appointment: restore to waiting — 20 October 2026
Approved on feat/restore-missed-visits from dcd1e4a. Staff queue offers Restore to waiting for a recorded missed visit, using the existing focused attendance confirmation with original patient/doctor/time/token and an explicit assessment-consent note. Cancel preserves Missed; Confirm restores the same booking/slot/queue/time, refreshes queue and clears review. Polling checks both status and missed event identity; doctor/date changes clear review. No mobile UI change: existing status polling shows Waiting and hides missed guidance.
Staff-only POST /queue/{id}/restore-waiting requires bounded missed_event_id. Missed records expose their latest missed audit ID. BEGIN IMMEDIATE serialises correction, current-state/episode checks reject stale or wrong confirmations, and missed_restorations links each missed event to one missed-restored audit event. Duplicate waiting-state retries create no extra event; called/completed/cancelled and unrelated waiting visits rejected. New audit link table only; no booking rebuild. Revoked assessment grants remain revoked. Another active follow-up blocks restoring an old follow-up; after cancelling the replacement, restoration makes the older active visit preferred in follow_up_record and prevents another duplicate booking. Current clinic/doctor/report/CSV counts update from saved status automatically.

## Patient booking: next available date - 21 October 2026
Approved after user chose the proposed patient-booking shortcut. Implemented on feat/patient-next-available-date from 660dc75. Book a visit offers Next available date after doctor suggestions. ## Prevent overlapping patient appointments - 22 October 2026
Approved after user selected this alternative to the proposed reschedule date filter (that filter was not implemented). Implemented on feat/patient-appointment-overlaps from a453423. A patient cannot create a new Waiting visit whose scheduled interval overlaps another Waiting or Called visit, across either doctor. Completed, cancelled and missed visits do not block. Half-open intervals allow a visit to start exactly when another ends; SQLite julianday compares timezone offsets and full stored slot durations correctly. Shared check runs inside existing BEGIN IMMEDIATE transactions for patient/counter/follow-up booking, rescheduling and restoring a missed visit. Rescheduling excludes its own original record, checks other visits before any mutation and keeps existing retry handling; missed-restoration retries/episode guards remain intact. No schema migration, deletion or retroactive modification of existing visits.
Conflict returns409 with: This patient already has an active visit that overlaps this time. Choose another time. No bookings were changed. Existing screens show the server message at confirmation; slot lists and Next available date do not prefilter cross-slot patient overlaps. Mobile booking/cancellation action errors now have separate state from connection errors, so background polling cannot erase the conflict message. Choosing another time/Back, navigation, patient switch or another attempt clears it. Connection-refresh failures retain Retry connection, including refresh failures after a successful action. RescheduleView already retains its own error. No dashboard code/shared type changes.

## Inventory: per-item low-stock alert levels - 22 October 2026
User approved the proposed per-item thresholds. Implemented on feat/item-stock-thresholds from 40f13af. Staff Pharmacy stock cards display each item's saved alert level and provide Set alert level with review/confirm, Keep editing, cancel and Reload latest item. Whole levels 0-99,999; 0 means alert only when empty. Low stock only filter and card labels use each item's own level, including out-of-stock items and preserving existing status filters. Inventory analysis uses positive-stock-at-or-below-own-level for low count, zero for out count, active-only alerts, and lists each alert's level. Historical period activity unchanged; inactive items retain editable levels but remain excluded from report alerts. Patient and doctor workflows unchanged.

Staff-only POST /medicines/{id}/threshold accepts strict integer level, catalogue version and request ID; extra fields rejected. BEGIN IMMEDIATE serialises changes with name/price/status edits, stale versions fail without writes, identical retries return saved results, mismatched retries fail. Retained medicine_threshold_changes audit captures old/new levels, version, actor/time/result. Additive medicine column defaults existing/new items to5, constrained0-99999. ## Inventory: tracked batch dispensing and live balances - 22 October 2026
User approved selecting batches when dispensing prescriptions. Implemented on feat/batch-dispensing from a4e1ca1. Staff Review dispensing now loads per-item stock sources and allows splitting required quantities across tracked receipt batches/untracked stock. Exact whole totals must match every prescribed item; current total/source limits and expiry rechecked on server. Expired means expiry<current Malaysia date; batches expiring today remain usable through that date. Default existing-client dispense uses only untracked stock and cannot silently consume tracked/expired batches. Staff-only stock-sources endpoint also returns immutable saved allocation snapshots. Staff can View saved stock allocations after dispensing; old prescriptions state historical source allocations not recorded. Patient/doctor Care responses unchanged.

Migration deliberately does not reconstruct historical balances: all existing stock/receipts stay untracked, even old receipts with expiry metadata. New receipts with paired batch/date insert receipt_balances atomically; new receipts without metadata join untracked stock. Untracked=medicine stock minus sum tracked live balances. Dispensing writes dispensing_allocations (medicine/receipt/quantity/batch+expiry snapshots/actor/time), decrements tracked balances and global stock and marks prescription/event in one immediate transaction. Repeated identical source allocations return original result; mismatched allocations after dispense409. All totals/sources validated before allocation writes. Staff role enforced. No FEFO auto-selection or retroactive allocation.

Batch-linked losses now also decrement known live balances; unassigned and historical-receipt losses can consume only untracked units. Original receipt loss allowance retained for audit, with explicit nullable live remaining_units. Loss UI checks both caps and distinguishes tracked live vs older untracked quantities. Expiry review shows remaining units for tracked receipts and excludes tracked balances0 from rows/counts; unknown historical receipts remain for physical review. UI/receiving wording updated. Sources refresh3seconds, input totals validated, source depletion/expiry while reviewing blocks confirmation but lets staff clear an invalid allocation. Dispensing action errors remain in child state across polling; choosing quantities/reopening clears them. New source data/allocations stay staff-only.

## Doctor to staff: Consultation checkout handover - 25 October 2026
Implemented the approved priority workflow on feat/consultation-checkout-handover from audit commit f13d073. Assigned doctor publishes a consultation note, issues any needed prescription, then reviews/confirms Finish consultation. Prescription is optional. Staff queue displays Ready for checkout while the underlying visit remains Called; staff retain Complete visit, dispensing and billing. Existing/legacy Called visits can still be completed by staff without this flag. Doctor cannot use staff completion. No new patient action is needed; existing completed visit details, care, simulated payment and feedback continue.

POST /visits/{booking_id}/consultation/finish requires the assigned doctor, Called status and a published note (private draft alone is insufficient). New consultation_handoffs table stores one immutable handover per visit with doctor/note/optional prescription IDs and finished_at. Transactional event consultation-finished and idempotent retries, including after staff completion. Booking metadata adds optional consultation_finished_at; staff receive operational timestamp only, no clinical note text. Finish does not complete the visit, dispense, bill or notify. New prescription after handover is rejected; identical existing prescription retries remain valid. Doctor care UI hides new prescription creation after finish. Published note amendments and other existing workflows retain their rules.

## Prescription directions corrections - 26 October 2026
Approved on feat/prescription-directions-corrections from 5cb09f6. Assigned doctors can correct directions only while the visit is Called, before consultation handover, dispensing or billing. Medicines, quantities, stored prices and prescription identity remain unchanged; medicine/quantity corrections are deferred for discussion. A reason, before/after review and explicit confirmation are required; cancel writes nothing. Doctor-only history retains previous/new directions, reason, author and time. Patients see latest directions and revision/time in Care and Visit details; staff see latest directions without private correction reasons/history. Existing prescription notification opens current data; correction does not add a notification or reset Read.

New empty sidecar table prescription_direction_revisions is the 36th table; legacy prescriptions remain revision 1. GET /visits/{booking_id}/prescription/directions-history and POST /visits/{booking_id}/prescription/directions enforce role/assignment and strict directions-only input. Corrections are transactional, versioned and idempotent for an exact latest retry; stale/no-op edits are rejected. A single SELECT reads directions and revision atomically to prevent mixed snapshots. Revised prescriptions require explicit current prescription_version when dispensing; legacy revision-1 requests remain compatible. Staff review captures the revision; polling detects a correction, disables confirmation/stock controls and requires cancel/review again. Doctor polling cannot overwrite the saved correction with an earlier in-flight response.

## Prescription cancellation - 26 October 2026
Approved on feat/prescription-cancellation from 7ddbebb. Assigned doctor can cancel an issued prescription only while the consultation is Called/open, before handover, dispensing or billing. Enter reason (1-300 characters), review visit/items/revision/consequences, then explicitly confirm. Keep prescription unchanged dismisses without writing. Immutable cancellation retains original medicine/quantity/directions/prices and doctor/reason/reviewed-version/time; exact retry returns the saved result, other cancellation content cannot replace it. Replacement/reissue and undo are intentionally unavailable in this task; UI explains this before confirmation. Directions corrections remain separate and are blocked after cancellation.

Implementation: new prescription_cancellations sidecar is table37. Existing prescriptions table/status CHECK and original rows remain unchanged; Store.prescription uses the same atomic SELECT to overlay effective status cancelled and cancelled_at from the sidecar. Future consumers must use this effective prescription status rather than interpreting raw issued rows alone. GET /visits/{booking_id}/prescription/cancellation exposes reason/audit only to assigned doctor; POST /visits/{booking_id}/prescription/cancel validates strict version/reason/extra-field rejection and saves sidecar/event transactionally. No new notification or Read reset. Patients/staff receive status/time, not reason. Cancellation/dispensing/handover serialize under BEGIN IMMEDIATE; cancelled prescriptions reject dispensing, carry no prescription into subsequent handover, and are excluded from invoice items/reviewed prescription ID. Consultation-only billing/payment remains possible. Patient Care/Visit details, doctor clinical history, staff checklist/reviews and status labels show Cancelled/historical items. Staff polling removes an open dispensing review after cancellation.

Verification: final all352APItests passed in155.765s (tmp/prescription-cancellation-api-tests.log), including10newtests for permissions/validation/preservation/reopening/privacy/retries/locks/rollback/stale revisions and concurrent cancellation vs dispensing/handover. ## Admin: deactivate/reactivate added staff - 29 October 2026
Approved on feat/admin-staff-status from269d57d. Admin /users shows Active/Inactive for added staff and protects original Staff Demo. Select Deactivate/Reactivate -> review exact staffID/current/new state -> confirm, or cancel without writes. Status author/time/version displayed; polling detects a competing status version and disables stale confirmation. Existing basic-information editing/creation flows retained. Original demo identities, patients/doctors and unknown IDs cannot be targeted. No deletion, role change, real login or patient/doctor deactivation.

New staff_status sidecar(table43) defaults added staff to active without rewriting profiles; staff_status_changes(table44) retains admin/time/from-version/target state/requestID. Both reference staff_creations so original identities cannot be changed. GET /admin/staff-status and POST /admin/staff/{staff_id}/status require admin. Strict boolean/version/request validation; BEGIN IMMEDIATE serializes changes, same request retries save once, mismatched/stale requests reject. An old exact retry returns current status and cannot undo a later reactivation. Status/history failure rolls both back. Basic profile versions and historical operational/clinical records are independent and retained.

Inactive staff are excluded from persisted role admission and /demo/staff, rejecting identity-protected API reads/writes with401. Direct staff guards and batch-hold helper also check status. Public health/slot discovery stays public. Store.connect rechecks the request's staff status before committing writes, closing the gap between dependency admission and a later deactivation; denied writes roll back. This preserves existing request-scoped action attribution/transactions. Deactivation and operational writes serialize through SQLite; already committed actions remain recorded.

Dashboard roster refresh10seconds removes inactive selection, clears stored selection, switches to protected Staff Demo with an explicit notice, and closes operational forms. Care fallback now updates an already selected staff role when the global identity resets; schedules already react to that change. User own-information selector resets an unavailable staff identity. Reactivation restores the same ID/profile to selectors; it does not automatically reselect it. Demo identity switching remains synthetic, not secure login. Added staff awaiting their first status refresh are labelled refreshing, rather than protected.

New immutable assessment references the selected previous assessment, with separate photo/symptoms/prediction snapshots. Cancel/discard creates no link. Parent result offers latest20direct follow-ups; history marks linked follow-ups; saved child can open its previous assessment or compare. Chains compare the immediately selected previous record. No retroactive linking/editing of existing assessments.

New assessment_followups sidecar(table45), childPK/parentFK/self-linkCHECK/index. Optional previous_id multipart field on existing POST /assessments requires fresh symptoms, verifies patient ownership before photo checking/prediction and again within the write transaction. Existing photo checker/model path remains unchanged; symptoms/previous record are not passed to the image-only predictor. Link and new assessment commit atomically; retry must match photo, symptoms and previousID, so retries cannot detach/reparent a record. GET /assessments/{assessment_id}/comparison returns only the owning patient's pair with photos; metadata carries follow_up_of, detail additionally carries latest20direct follow-up IDs/dates. Original schema/rows and historical prediction snapshots retained.

Doctor review adds Compare with previous assessment. GET /assessment-shares/{share_id}/comparison requires the current share plus a separate active, authorised share of the previous assessment to the same doctor. Reuses appointment validity/ownership/revocation checks, trying another valid parent share when an appointment-linked share is no longer valid. Sharing a follow-up never shares the previous photo automatically. No parent photo/symptoms/prediction returned without both permissions. Open comparison polls5seconds and clears both comparison records on revocation/error; current independently authorised assessment stays open. Existing clinical-assessment/draft/publish workflow remains per assessment. No new review-writing or notification mechanism.

Patient comparison displays previous/current dated photos, patient-reported symptoms and each independent AI result, side by side on wide screens and stacked on narrow screens. Guidance explains that model scores cannot measure improvement/worsening. No trend/diagnosis inference, model replacement/retraining or evaluation claim. Sharing each record remains explicit. Real login, Word report and replacement prescriptions remain deferred; staff activity viewer was proposed but not approved.

## Skin assessment: doctor photo follow-up requests - 29 October 2026
Approved on feat/photo-followup-requests from097c183. On a shared assessment, doctor has an independent Photo follow-up request panel: choose future Malaysia date/instructions ->review ->confirm, or keep editing without writes. One fixed published request per share; instructions/date are readonly after confirmation and survive reload. This does not publish a clinical review, book a visit, schedule timed reminders or issue phone push. Real login, model replacement/retraining, Word report and replacement prescriptions remain deferred.

New photo_followup_requests(table46): UUID, unique shareID, due date, instructions, creation time and unique client requestID. GET/POST /assessment-shares/{share_id}/photo-follow-up-request require current assigned-doctor share/appointment access. Strict input/extra-field rejection, future Malaysia date within365days, bounded nonblank multiline instructions. BEGIN IMMEDIATE serializes save/revocation; exact normalized retries save once, mismatched requests/another request on same share409. Retry of an already saved request remains valid after its date passes, but revoked access still blocks doctor reads/writes. Request and photo_follow_up_requested notification save atomically; notification failure rolls request back. One immediate inbox notification targets source assessmentID and requestUUID, with doctor/date/guidance but no private instruction text. Retries preserve Read. Clinical-review/draft/assessment/model data are not rewritten.

GET /assessments/{assessment_id}/photo-follow-up-requests is patient-owned, latest100 or targeted photo_request_id. Patient retains published request history after share revocation, like published review history; it does not grant the doctor renewed access. App adds Photo follow-up requests filter/Open photo request routing to source assessment, displaying doctor/date/instructions. Targeted lookup handles an older request outside latest100 and rejects cross-assessment/patient targets. Start requested photo follow-up enters the existing linked-photo workflow, showing request context; patient must choose a new photo and enter fresh symptoms. New assessments are not auto-shared or treated as automatic request completion. Cancel clears the workflow and explicitly reports no assessment saved. Back retains inbox filters/Read; patient switch clears scoped routing. Empty/header guidance includes the new notification type.
